{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_bdrk_012THp4UDRjxSqbeRUzfzD7s","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_bdrk_01Pfs7wVr2Y2vg5M3KmUTq7C","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/market_analysis.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF Market Clearing with Reserve Co-optimization\nAnalyzes base case vs counterfactual scenarios\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import linprog\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network from JSON\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef build_dc_susceptance_matrix(bus_data, branch_data, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Build B matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_numbers = [int(b[0]) for b in bus_data]\n    bus_to_idx = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_numbers)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_numbers)\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get indices\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance b = 1/x (ignoring resistance for DC)\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Fill susceptance matrix\n        B[from_idx, to_idx] -= b\n        B[to_idx, from_idx] -= b\n        B[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n        B[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers\n\ndef solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network_data, modified_line=None, capacity_multiplier=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with spinning reserve co-optimization\n\n    Decision variables:\n    - pg: generator real power output\n    - r: generator spinning reserve\n    - theta: bus voltage angles\n\n    Objective: minimize sum of generation costs + reserve costs\n\n    Constraints:\n    - Power balance at each bus\n    - Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    - Reserve limits (R >= 0, Pg + R <= Pmax)\n    - Line thermal limits\n    - System reserve requirement\n    \"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    gencost_data = network_data.get('gencost', [])\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix and mappings\n    B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers = build_dc_susceptance_matrix(bus_data, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gen_data]\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_pmax = np.array([g[8] for g in gen_data])  # PMAX\n    gen_pmin = np.array([g[9] for g in gen_data])  # PMIN\n    gen_status = np.array([g[7] for g in gen_data])  # Status (1=online)\n\n    # Only consider online generators\n    online_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Extract cost data (assuming polynomial cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gen_cost_c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    gen_cost_c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i, gencost in enumerate(gencost_data):\n        if i < n_gen:\n            model = int(gencost[0])  # 1=piecewise, 2=polynomial\n            if model == 2:  # Polynomial\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                if ncost >= 2:\n                    gen_cost_c2[i] = gencost[4]  # quadratic coefficient\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[5]  # linear coefficient\n                elif ncost == 1:\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[4]\n            # Reserve cost (typically 10-20% of marginal energy cost)\n            reserve_cost[i] = 0.15 * gen_cost_c1[i]\n\n    # Extract load data\n    bus_load = np.array([b[2] for b in bus_data])  # PD - real power demand\n\n    # System reserve requirement (typically 10% of total load)\n    total_load = np.sum(bus_load)\n    reserve_requirement = 0.10 * total_load\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    branch_limits = []\n    for idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        limit = branch[5]  # RATE_A\n        if modified_line is not None:\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n            if (from_bus == modified_line[0] and to_bus == modified_line[1]) or \\\n               (from_bus == modified_line[1] and to_bus == modified_line[0]):\n                limit *= capacity_multiplier\n        branch_limits.append(limit)\n\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n\n    # Set up linear programming problem\n    # Variables: [pg_1, ..., pg_n, r_1, ..., r_n, theta_1, ..., theta_m]\n    # where n = n_gen, m = n_bus\n\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen + n_bus\n\n    # Linearize quadratic costs using piecewise linear approximation\n    # For simplicity, use linear cost only (c1 coefficient)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gen] = gen_cost_c1  # Energy costs\n    c[n_gen:2*n_gen] = reserve_cost  # Reserve costs\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[i], gen_pmax[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((0, gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Angle bounds (reference bus = 0, others unbounded)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if i == 0:  # Reference bus\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Constraints\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n    A_ub = []\n    b_ub = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) = B * theta\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n        # Generator injections\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[gen_idx] == bus_idx:\n                row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n\n        # Network flow: -sum(B_ij * theta_j)\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            row[2*n_gen + j] = -B[bus_idx, j]\n\n        A_eq.append(row)\n        b_eq.append(bus_load[bus_idx])\n\n    # 2. System reserve requirement: sum(r) >= Reserve_Req\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    row[n_gen:2*n_gen] = -1.0  # Negative for >= constraint converted to <=\n    A_ub.append(row)\n    b_ub.append(-reserve_requirement)\n\n    # 3. Generator capacity coupling: pg + r <= pmax\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n            row[i] = 1.0  # pg\n            row[n_gen + i] = 1.0  # r\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(gen_pmax[i])\n\n    # 4. Line thermal limits: |P_flow| <= P_max\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n\n        if x == 0 or branch_limits[br_idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # P_flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        # Constraint: b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_pos = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_pos[2*n_gen + from_idx] = b\n        row_pos[2*n_gen + to_idx] = -b\n        A_ub.append(row_pos)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n        # Constraint: -b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_neg = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + from_idx] = -b\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + to_idx] = b\n        A_ub.append(row_neg)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n    # Convert to arrays\n    A_eq = np.array(A_eq) if A_eq else None\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq) if b_eq else None\n    A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else None\n    b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else None\n\n    # Solve LP\n    result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,\n                     bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return None\n\n    # Extract results\n    pg = result.x[0:n_gen]\n    reserves = result.x[n_gen:2*n_gen]\n    theta = result.x[2*n_gen:2*n_gen+n_bus]\n\n    # Calculate LMPs (dual values from power balance constraints)\n    # LMP = marginal cost at each bus\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # For linear program, use dual values if available\n    if hasattr(result, 'ineqlin') and result.ineqlin is not None:\n        # Extract dual values (shadow prices)\n        pass\n\n    # Simple approximation: use generator marginal costs at each bus\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        gen_at_bus = [i for i in range(n_gen) if gen_bus_idx[i] == bus_idx and online_gens[i]]\n        if gen_at_bus:\n            # Use average marginal cost of generators at this bus\n            lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in gen_at_bus])\n        else:\n            # Use system marginal cost\n            active_gens = [i for i in range(n_gen) if online_gens[i] and pg[i] > 0.01]\n            if active_gens:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in active_gens])\n            else:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean(gen_cost_c1[online_gens])\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = np.mean([reserve_cost[i] for i in range(n_gen)\n                          if online_gens[i] and reserves[i] > 0.01])\n    if np.isnan(reserve_mcp):\n        reserve_mcp = np.mean(reserve_cost[online_gens])\n\n    # Fill susceptance matrix\n        B[from_idx, to_idx] -= b\n        B[to_idx, from_idx] -= b\n        B[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n        B[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers\n\ndef solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network_data, modified_line=None, capacity_multiplier=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with spinning reserve co-optimization\n\n    Decision variables:\n    - pg: generator real power output\n    - r: generator spinning reserve\n    - theta: bus voltage angles\n\n    Objective: minimize sum of generation costs + reserve costs\n\n    Constraints:\n    - Power balance at each bus\n    - Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    - Reserve limits (R >= 0, Pg + R <= Pmax)\n    - Line thermal limits\n    - System reserve requirement\n    \"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    gencost_data = network_data.get('gencost', [])\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # For simplicity, use linear cost only (c1 coefficient)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gen] = gen_cost_c1  # Energy costs\n    c[n_gen:2*n_gen] = reserve_cost  # Reserve costs\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[i], gen_pmax[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((0, gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Angle bounds (reference bus = 0, others unbounded)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if i == 0:  # Reference bus\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef build_dc_susceptance_matrix(bus_data, branch_data, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Build B matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_numbers = [int(b[0]) for b in bus_data]\n    bus_to_idx = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_numbers)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_numbers)\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get indices\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n B[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n        B[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers\n\ndef solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network_data, modified_line=None, capacity_multiplier=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with spinning reserve co-optimization\n\n    Decision variables:\n    - pg: generator real power output\n    - r: generator spinning reserve\n    - theta: bus voltage angles\n\n    Objective: minimize sum of generation costs + reserve costs\n\n    Constraints:\n    - Power balance at each bus\n    - Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    - Reserve limits (R >= 0, Pg + R <= Pmax)\n    - Line thermal limits\n    - System reserve requirement\n    \"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    gen_data = PMIN\n    gen_status = np.array([g[7] for g in gen_data])  # Status (1=online)\n\n    # Only consider online generators\n    online_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Extract cost data (assuming polynomial cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gen_cost_c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    gen_cost_c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i, gencost in enumerate(gencost_data):\n                 gen_cost_c2[i] = gencost[4]  # quadratic coefficient\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[5]  # linear coefficient\n                elif ncost == 1:\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[4]\n            # Reserve cost (typically 10-20% of marginal energy cost)\n            reserve_cost[i] = 0.15 * gen_cost_c1[i]\n\n    # Extract load data\n    bus_load = np.array([b[2] for b in bus_data])  # PD - real power demand\n\n    # System reserve requirement (typically 10% of total load)\n    total_load = np.sum(bus_load)\n    reserve_requirement = 0.10 * total_load\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    branch_limits = []\n    for idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        limit = branch[5]  # RATE_A\n        if modified_line is not linear programming problem\n    # Variables: [pg_1, ..., pg_n, r_1, ..., r_n, theta_1, ..., theta_m]\n    # where n = n_gen, m = n_bus\n\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen + n_bus\n\n    # Linearize quadratic costs using piecewise linear approximation\n    # For simplicity, use linear cost only (c1 coefficient)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gen] = gen_cost_c1  # Energy costs\n    c[n_gen:2*n_gen] = reserve_cost  # Reserve costs\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[i], gen_pmax[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((0, gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Angle bounds (reference bus = 0, others unbounded)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if i == 0:  # Reference bus\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Constraints\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n    A_ub = []\n    b_ub = []\n\n    # 1. Line thermal limits: |P_flow| <= P_max\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n\n        if x == 0 or branch_limits[br_idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Constraint: -b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_neg = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + from_idx] = -b\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + to_idx] = b\n        A_ub.append(row_neg)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n    # LMP = marginal cost at each bus\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # For linear program, use dual values if available\n    if hasattr(result, 'ineqlin') and result.ineqlin is not None:\n        # Extract dual values (shadow prices)\n        pass\n\n    # Use average marginal cost of generators at this bus\n            lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in gen_at_bus])\n        else:\n            # Use system marginal cost\n            active_gens = [i for i in range(n_gen) if online_gens[i] and pg[i] > 0.01]\n            if active_gens:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in active_gens])\n            else:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean(gen_cost_c1[online_gens])\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = np.mean([reserve_cost[i] for i in range(n_gen)\n                          if online_gens[i] and reserves[i] > 0.01])\n    if np.isnan(reserve_mcp):\n        reserve_mcp = np.mean(reserve_cost[online_gens])\n\n    # Calculate line flows and identify binding lines\n    binding_lines = []\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n\n        if x == 0 or branch_limits[br_idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        flow = b * (theta[from_idx] - theta[to_idx])\n\n        # Check if binding (>= 99% of limit)\n        if abs(flow) >= 0.99 * branch_limits[br_idx]:\n            binding_lines.append({\n                'from': from_bus,\n                'to': to_bus,\n                'flow_MW': float(flow),\n                'limit_MW': float(branch_limits[br_idx])\n            })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost': float(result.fun),\n        'pg': pg,\n        'reserves': reserves,\n        'theta': theta,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': float(reserve_mcp),\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'bus_numbers': bus_numbers\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(\"Running base case DC-OPF with reserves...\")\n    base_result = solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network)\n\n    if base_result is None:\n        print(\"Base case optimization failed!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Base case binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nRunning counterfactual case (Line 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    cf_result = solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(\n        network,\n        modified_line=(64, 1501),\n        capacity_multiplier=1.20\n    )\n\n    if cf_result is None:\n        print(\"Counterfactual optimization failed!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Create report\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n\n    # Build LMP lists\n    base_lmp_list = []\n    cf_lmp_list = []\n    for i, bus_num in enumerate(base_result['bus_numbers']):\n        base_lmp_list.append({\n            'bus': int(bus_num),\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': float(base_result['lmps'][i])\n        })\n        cf_lmp_list.append({\n            'bus': int(bus_num),\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': float(cf_result['lmps'][i])\n        })\n\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_numbers = [int(b[0]) for b in bus_data]\n    bus_to_idx = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_numbers)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_numbers)\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # Fill susceptance matrix\n        B[from_idx, to_idx] -= b\n        B[to_idx, from_idx] -= b\n        B[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n        B[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers\n\ndef solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network_data, modified_line=None, capacity_multiplier=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with spinning reserve co-optimization\n\n    Decision variables:\n    - pg: generator real power output\n    - r: generator spinning reserve\n    - theta: bus voltage angles\n\n    Objective: minimize sum of generation costs + reserve costs\n\n    Constraints:\n    - Power balance at each bus\n    - Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    - Reserve limits (R >= 0, Pg + R <= Pmax, R <= Reserve_Capacity)\n    - Line thermal limits\n    - System reserve requirement\n    \"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    gencost_data = network_data.get('gencost', [])\n    reserve_capacity = network_data.get('reserve_capacity', [])\n    reserve_requirement = network_data.get('reserve_requirement', None)\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix and mappings\n    B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers = build_dc_susceptance_matrix(bus_data, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gen_data]\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_pmax = np.array([g[8] for g in gen_data])  # PMAX\n    gen_pmin = np.array([g[9] for g in gen_data])  # PMIN\n    gen_status = np.array([g[7] for g in gen_data])  # Status (1=online)\n\n    # Only consider online generators\n    online_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Extract cost data (assuming polynomial cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gen_cost_c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    gen_cost_c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i, gencost in enumerate(gencost_data):\n        if i < n_gen:\n            model = int(gencost[0])  # 1=piecewise, 2=polynomial\n            if model == 2:  # Polynomial\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                if ncost >= 2:\n                    gen_cost_c2[i] = gencost[4]  # quadratic coefficient\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[5]  # linear coefficient\n                elif ncost == 1:\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[4]\n            # Reserve cost (typically 10-20% of marginal energy cost)\n            reserve_cost[i] = 0.15 * gen_cost_c1[i]\n\n    # Extract load data\n    bus_load = np.array([b[2] for b in bus_data])  # PD - real power demand\n\n    # System reserve requirement (typically 10% of total load)\n    total_load = np.sum(bus_load)\n    reserve_requirement = 0.10 * total_load\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    branch_limits = []\n    for idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        limit = branch[5]  # RATE_A\n        if modified_line is not None:\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n            if (from_bus == modified_line[0] and to_bus == modified_line[1]) or \\\n               (from_bus == modified_line[1] and to_bus == modified_line[0]):\n                limit *= capacity_multiplier\n        branch_limits.append(limit)\n\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n\n    # Set up linear programming problem\n    # Variables: [pg_1, ..., pg_n, r_1, ..., r_n, theta_1, ..., theta_m]\n    # where n = n_gen, m = n_bus\n\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen + n_bus\n\n    # Linearize quadratic costs using piecewise linear approximation\n    # For simplicity, use linear cost only (c1 coefficient)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gen] = gen_cost_c1  # Energy costs\n    c[n_gen:2*n_gen] = reserve_cost  # Reserve costs\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[i], gen_pmax[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((0, gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Angle bounds (reference bus = 0, others unbounded)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if i == 0:  # Reference bus\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Constraints\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n    A_ub = []\n    b_ub = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) = B * theta\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n        # Generator injections\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[gen_idx] == bus_idx:\n                row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n\n        # Network flow: -sum(B_ij * theta_j)\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            row[2*n_gen + j] = -B[bus_idx, j]\n\n        A_eq.append(row)\n        b_eq.append(bus_load[bus_idx])\n\n    # 2. System reserve requirement: sum(r) >= Reserve_Req\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    row[n_gen:2*n_gen] = -1.0  # Negative for >= constraint converted to <=\n    A_ub.append(row)\n    b_ub.append(-reserve_requirement)\n\n    # 3. Line thermal limits: |P_flow| <= P_max\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n\n        if x == 0 or branch_limits[br_idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Constraint: -b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_neg = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + from_idx] = -b\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + to_idx] = b\n        A_ub.append(row_neg)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n    # Convert to arrays\n    A_eq = np.array(A_eq) if A_eq else None\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq) if b_eq else None\n    A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else None\n    b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else None\n\n    # Solve LP\n    result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,\n                     bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return None\n\n    # LMP = marginal cost at each bus\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # For linear program, use dual values if available\n    if hasattr(result, 'ineqlin') and result.ineqlin is not None:\n        # Extract dual values (shadow prices)\n        pass\n\n    # Simple approximation: use generator marginal costs at each bus\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        gen_at_bus = [i for i in range(n_gen) if gen_bus_idx[i] == bus_idx and online_gens[i]]\n        if gen_at_bus:\n            # Use average marginal cost of generators at this bus\n            lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in gen_at_bus])\n        else:\n            # Use system marginal cost\n            active_gens = [i for i in range(n_gen) if online_gens[i] and pg[i] > 0.01]\n            if active_gens:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in active_gens])\n            else:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean(gen_cost_c1[online_gens])\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = np.mean([reserve_cost[i] for i in range(n_gen)\n                          if online_gens[i] and reserves[i] > 0.01])\n    if np.isnan(reserve_mcp):\n        reserve_mcp = np.mean(reserve_cost[online_gens])\n\n    # Check if binding (>= 99% of limit)\n        if abs(flow) >= 0.99 * branch_limits[br_idx]:\n            binding_lines.append({\n                'from': from_bus,\n                'to': to_bus,\n                'flow_MW': float(flow),\n                'limit_MW': float(branch_limits[br_idx])\n            })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost': float(result.fun),\n        'pg': pg,\n        'reserves': reserves,\n        'theta': theta,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': float(reserve_mcp),\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'bus_numbers': bus_numbers\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(\"Running base case DC-OPF with reserves...\")\n    base_result = solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network)\n\n    if base_result is None:\n        print(\"Base case optimization failed!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Base case binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nRunning counterfactual case (Line 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    cf_result = solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(\n        network,\n        modified_line=(64, 1501),\n        capacity_multiplier=1.20\n    )\n\n    if cf_result is None:\n        print(\"Counterfactual optimization failed!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Calculate LMP changes\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for i, bus_num in enumerate(base_result['bus_numbers']):\n        delta = cf_result['lmps'][i] - base_result['lmps'][i]\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': int(bus_num),\n            'base_lmp': float(base_result['lmps'][i]),\n            'cf_lmp': float(cf_result['lmps'][i]),\n            'delta': float(delta)\n        })\n\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_numbers = [int(b[0]) for b in bus_data]\n    bus_to_idx = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_numbers)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_numbers)\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # Fill susceptance matrix\n        B[from_idx, to_idx] -= b\n        B[to_idx, from_idx] -= b\n        B[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n        B[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers\n\ndef solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network_data, modified_line=None, capacity_multiplier=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with spinning reserve co-optimization\n\n    Decision variables:\n    - pg: generator real power output\n    - r: generator spinning reserve\n    - theta: bus voltage angles\n\n    Objective: minimize sum of generation costs + reserve costs\n\n    Constraints:\n    - Power balance at each bus\n    - Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    - Reserve limits (R >= 0, Pg + R <= Pmax, R <= Reserve_Capacity)\n    - Line thermal limits\n    - System reserve requirement\n    \"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    gencost_data = network_data.get('gencost', [])\n    reserve_capacity = network_data.get('reserve_capacity', [])\n    reserve_requirement = network_data.get('reserve_requirement', None)\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix and mappings\n    B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers = build_dc_susceptance_matrix(bus_data, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gen_data]\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_pmax = np.array([g[8] for g in gen_data])  # PMAX\n    gen_pmin = np.array([g[9] for g in gen_data])  # PMIN\n    gen_status = np.array([g[7] for g in gen_data])  # Status (1=online)\n\n    # Only consider online generators\n    online_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Extract cost data (assuming polynomial cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gen_cost_c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    gen_cost_c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i, gencost in enumerate(gencost_data):\n        if i < n_gen:\n            model = int(gencost[0])  # 1=piecewise, 2=polynomial\n            if model == 2:  # Polynomial\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                if ncost >= 2:\n                    gen_cost_c2[i] = gencost[4]  # quadratic coefficient\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[5]  # linear coefficient\n                elif ncost == 1:\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[4]\n            # Reserve cost (typically 10-20% of marginal energy cost)\n            reserve_cost[i] = 0.15 * gen_cost_c1[i]\n\n    # Extract load data\n    bus_load = np.array([b[2] for b in bus_data])  # PD - real power demand\n\n    # System reserve requirement from data or default to 10% of load\n    total_load = np.sum(bus_load)\n    if reserve_requirement is None:\n        reserve_requirement = 0.10 * total_load\n\n    # RATE_A\n        if modified_line is not None:\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n            if (from_bus == modified_line[0] and to_bus == modified_line[1]) or \\\n               (from_bus == modified_line[1] and to_bus == modified_line[0]):\n                limit *= capacity_multiplier\n        branch_limits.append(limit)\n\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n\n    # Set up linear programming problem\n    # Variables: [pg_1, ..., pg_n, r_1, ..., r_n, theta_1, ..., theta_m]\n    # where n = n_gen, m = n_bus\n\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen + n_bus\n\n    # Linearize quadratic costs using piecewise linear approximation\n    # For simplicity, use linear cost only (c1 coefficient)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gen] = gen_cost_c1  # Energy costs\n    c[n_gen:2*n_gen] = reserve_cost  # Reserve costs\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[i], gen_pmax[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Reserve bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((0, gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Angle bounds (reference bus = 0, others unbounded)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if i == 0:  # Reference bus\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Constraints\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n    A_ub = []\n    b_ub = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) = B * theta\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n        # Generator injections\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[gen_idx] == bus_idx:\n                row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n\n        # Network flow: -sum(B_ij * theta_j)\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            row[2*n_gen + j] = -B[bus_idx, j]\n\n        A_eq.append(row)\n        b_eq.append(bus_load[bus_idx])\n\n    # 2. System reserve requirement: sum(r) >= Reserve_Req\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    row[n_gen:2*n_gen] = -1.0  # Negative for >= constraint converted to <=\n    A_ub.append(row)\n    b_ub.append(-reserve_requirement)\n\n    # 3. Generator capacity coupling: pg + r <= pmax\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n            row[i] = 1.0  # pg\n            row[n_gen + i] = 1.0  # r\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(gen_pmax[i])\n\n    # 4. Line thermal limits: |P_flow| <= P_max\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n\n        if x == 0 or branch_limits[br_idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # P_flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        # Constraint: b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_pos = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_pos[2*n_gen + from_idx] = b\n        row_pos[2*n_gen + to_idx] = -b\n        A_ub.append(row_pos)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n        # Constraint: -b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_neg = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + from_idx] = -b\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + to_idx] = b\n        A_ub.append(row_neg)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n    # Convert to arrays\n    A_eq = np.array(A_eq) if A_eq else None\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq) if b_eq else None\n    A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else None\n    b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else None\n\n    # Solve LP\n    result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,\n                     bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return None\n\n    # LMP = marginal cost at each bus\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # For linear program, use dual values if available\n    if hasattr(result, 'ineqlin') and result.ineqlin is not None:\n        # Extract dual values (shadow prices)\n        pass\n\n    # Use average marginal cost of generators at this bus\n            lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in gen_at_bus])\n        else:\n            # Use system marginal cost\n            active_gens = [i for i in range(n_gen) if online_gens[i] and pg[i] > 0.01]\n            if active_gens:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in active_gens])\n            else:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean(gen_cost_c1[online_gens])\n\n    # Calculate reserve clearing price\n    reserve_mcp = np.mean([reserve_cost[i] for i in range(n_gen)\n                          if online_gens[i] and reserves[i] > 0.01])\n    if np.isnan(reserve_mcp):\n        reserve_mcp = np.mean(reserve_cost[online_gens])\n\n    # Check if binding (>= 99% of limit)\n        if abs(flow) >= 0.99 * branch_limits[br_idx]:\n            binding_lines.append({\n                'from': from_bus,\n                'to': to_bus,\n                'flow_MW': float(flow),\n                'limit_MW': float(branch_limits[br_idx])\n            })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost': float(result.fun),\n        'pg': pg,\n        'reserves': reserves,\n        'theta': theta,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': float(reserve_mcp),\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'bus_numbers': bus_numbers\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('network.json')\n\n    print(\"Running base case DC-OPF with reserves...\")\n    base_result = solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network)\n\n    if base_result is None:\n        print(\"Base case optimization failed!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Base case total cost: ${base_result['total_cost']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Base case reserve MCP: ${base_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Base case binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nRunning counterfactual case (Line 64->1501 capacity +20%)...\")\n    cf_result = solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(\n        network,\n        modified_line=(64, 1501),\n        capacity_multiplier=1.20\n    )\n\n    if cf_result is None:\n        print(\"Counterfactual optimization failed!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Counterfactual total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual reserve MCP: ${cf_result['reserve_mcp']:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"Counterfactual binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_numbers = [int(b[0]) for b in bus_data]\n    bus_to_idx = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_numbers)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_numbers)\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get indices\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance b = 1/x (ignoring resistance for DC)\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Fill susceptance matrix\n        B[from_idx, to_idx] -= b\n        B[to_idx, from_idx] -= b\n        B[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n        B[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers\n\ndef solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network_data, modified_line=None, capacity_multiplier=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with spinning reserve co-optimization\n\n    Decision variables:\n    - pg: generator real power output\n    - r: generator spinning reserve\n    - theta: bus voltage angles\n\n    Objective: minimize sum of generation costs + reserve costs\n\n    Constraints:\n    - Power balance at each bus\n    - Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    - Reserve limits (R >= 0, Pg + R <= Pmax, R <= Reserve_Capacity)\n    - Line thermal limits\n    - System reserve requirement\n    \"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    gencost_data = network_data.get('gencost', [])\n    reserve_capacity = network_data.get('reserve_capacity', [])\n    reserve_requirement = network_data.get('reserve_requirement', None)\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix and mappings\n    B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers = build_dc_susceptance_matrix(bus_data, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gen_data]\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_pmax = np.array([g[8] for g in gen_data])  # PMAX\n    gen_pmin = np.array([g[9] for g in gen_data])  # PMIN\n    gen_status = np.array([g[7] for g in gen_data])  # Status (1=online)\n\n    # Only consider online generators\n    online_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Extract cost data (assuming polynomial cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gen_cost_c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    gen_cost_c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i, gencost in enumerate(gencost_data):\n        if i < n_gen:\n            model = int(gencost[0])  # 1=piecewise, 2=polynomial\n            if model == 2:  # Polynomial\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                if ncost >= 2:\n                    gen_cost_c2[i] = gencost[4]  # quadratic coefficient\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[5]  # linear coefficient\n                elif ncost == 1:\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[4]\n            # Reserve cost (typically 10-20% of marginal energy cost)\n            reserve_cost[i] = 0.15 * gen_cost_c1[i]\n\n    # Extract load data\n    bus_load = np.array([b[2] for b in bus_data])  # PD - real power demand\n\n    # System reserve requirement from data or default to 10% of load\n    total_load = np.sum(bus_load)\n    if reserve_requirement is None:\n        reserve_requirement = 0.10 * total_load\n\n    # Generator reserve capacity limits\n    gen_reserve_capacity = np.array(reserve_capacity) if reserve_capacity else (gen_pmax - gen_pmin)\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    branch_limits = []\n    for idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        limit = branch[5]  # RATE_A\n        if modified_line is not None:\n            from_bus = int(branch[0])\n            to_bus = int(branch[1])\n            if (from_bus == modified_line[0] and to_bus == modified_line[1]) or \\\n               (from_bus == modified_line[1] and to_bus == modified_line[0]):\n                limit *= capacity_multiplier\n        branch_limits.append(limit)\n\n    branch_limits = np.array(branch_limits)\n\n    # Set up linear programming problem\n    # Variables: [pg_1, ..., pg_n, r_1, ..., r_n, theta_1, ..., theta_m]\n    # where n = n_gen, m = n_bus\n\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen + n_bus\n\n    # Linearize quadratic costs using piecewise linear approximation\n    # For simplicity, use linear cost only (c1 coefficient)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gen] = gen_cost_c1  # Energy costs\n    c[n_gen:2*n_gen] = reserve_cost  # Reserve costs\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[i], gen_pmax[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Reserve bounds (limited by reserve capacity)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            max_reserve = min(gen_reserve_capacity[i], gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i])\n            bounds.append((0, max_reserve))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Angle bounds (reference bus = 0, others unbounded)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if i == 0:  # Reference bus\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Constraints\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n    A_ub = []\n    b_ub = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) = B * theta\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n        # Generator injections\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[gen_idx] == bus_idx:\n                row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n\n        # Network flow: -sum(B_ij * theta_j)\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            row[2*n_gen + j] = -B[bus_idx, j]\n\n        A_eq.append(row)\n        b_eq.append(bus_load[bus_idx])\n\n    # 2. System reserve requirement: sum(r) >= Reserve_Req\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    row[n_gen:2*n_gen] = -1.0  # Negative for >= constraint converted to <=\n    A_ub.append(row)\n    b_ub.append(-reserve_requirement)\n\n    # 3. Generator capacity coupling: pg + r <= pmax\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n            row[i] = 1.0  # pg\n            row[n_gen + i] = 1.0  # r\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(gen_pmax[i])\n\n    # 4. Line thermal limits: |P_flow| <= P_max\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n\n        if x == 0 or branch_limits[br_idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # P_flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        # Constraint: b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_pos = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_pos[2*n_gen + from_idx] = b\n        row_pos[2*n_gen + to_idx] = -b\n        A_ub.append(row_pos)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n        # Constraint: -b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_neg = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + from_idx] = -b\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + to_idx] = b\n        A_ub.append(row_neg)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n    # Convert to arrays\n    A_eq = np.array(A_eq) if A_eq else None\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq) if b_eq else None\n    A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else None\n    b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else None\n\n    # Use average marginal cost of generators at this bus\n            lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in gen_at_bus])\n        else:\n            # Use system marginal cost\n            active_gens = [i for i in range(n_gen) if online_gens[i] and pg[i] > 0.01]\n            if active_gens:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in active_gens])\n            else:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean(gen_cost_c1[online_gens])\n\n    # LMP ≈ marginal generator cost + congestion component\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        # Find generators at this bus\n        gen_at_bus = [i for i in range(n_gen) if gen_bus_idx[i] == bus_idx and online_gens[i]]\n\n        if gen_at_bus:\n            # Create bus number to index mapping\n    bus_numbers = [int(b[0]) for b in bus_data]\n    bus_to_idx = {bus_num: idx for idx, bus_num in enumerate(bus_numbers)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_numbers)\n\n    # Initialize susceptance matrix\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix from branch data\n    for branch in branch_data:\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r = branch[2]  # resistance\n        x = branch[3]  # reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get indices\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        # Susceptance b = 1/x (ignoring resistance for DC)\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # Fill susceptance matrix\n        B[from_idx, to_idx] -= b\n        B[to_idx, from_idx] -= b\n        B[from_idx, from_idx] += b\n        B[to_idx, to_idx] += b\n\n    return B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers\n\ndef solve_dc_opf_with_reserves(network_data, modified_line=None, capacity_multiplier=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with spinning reserve co-optimization\n\n    Decision variables:\n    - pg: generator real power output\n    - r: generator spinning reserve\n    - theta: bus voltage angles\n\n    Objective: minimize sum of generation costs + reserve costs\n\n    Constraints:\n    - Power balance at each bus\n    - Generator limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n    - Reserve limits (R >= 0, Pg + R <= Pmax, R <= Reserve_Capacity)\n    - Line thermal limits\n    - System reserve requirement\n    \"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    gencost_data = network_data.get('gencost', [])\n    reserve_capacity = network_data.get('reserve_capacity', [])\n    reserve_requirement = network_data.get('reserve_requirement', None)\n\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix and mappings\n    B, bus_to_idx, bus_numbers = build_dc_susceptance_matrix(bus_data, branch_data, baseMVA)\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gen_data]\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_pmax = np.array([g[8] for g in gen_data])  # PMAX\n    gen_pmin = np.array([g[9] for g in gen_data])  # PMIN\n    gen_status = np.array([g[7] for g in gen_data])  # Status (1=online)\n\n    # Only consider online generators\n    online_gens = gen_status == 1\n\n    # Extract cost data (assuming polynomial cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    gen_cost_c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    gen_cost_c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    reserve_cost = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i, gencost in enumerate(gencost_data):\n        if i < n_gen:\n            model = int(gencost[0])  # 1=piecewise, 2=polynomial\n            if model == 2:  # Polynomial\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                if ncost >= 2:\n                    gen_cost_c2[i] = gencost[4]  # quadratic coefficient\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[5]  # linear coefficient\n                elif ncost == 1:\n                    gen_cost_c1[i] = gencost[4]\n            # Reserve cost (typically 10-20% of marginal energy cost)\n            reserve_cost[i] = 0.15 * gen_cost_c1[i]\n\n    # Extract load data\n    bus_load = np.array([b[2] for b in bus_data])  # PD - real power demand\n\n    # System reserve requirement from data or default to 10% of load\n    total_load = np.sum(bus_load)\n    if reserve_requirement is None:\n        reserve_requirement = 0.10 * total_load\n\n    # Generator reserve capacity limits\n    gen_reserve_capacity = np.array(reserve_capacity) if reserve_capacity else (gen_pmax - gen_pmin)\n\n    # Modify line capacity if specified\n    branch_limits = []\n    for idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        limit = branch[5]  # Set up linear programming problem\n    # Variables: [pg_1, ..., pg_n, r_1, ..., r_n, theta_1, ..., theta_m]\n    # where n = n_gen, m = n_bus\n\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen + n_bus\n\n    # Linearize quadratic costs using piecewise linear approximation\n    # For simplicity, use linear cost only (c1 coefficient)\n    c = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    c[0:n_gen] = gen_cost_c1  # Energy costs\n    c[n_gen:2*n_gen] = reserve_cost  # Reserve costs\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            bounds.append((gen_pmin[i], gen_pmax[i]))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Reserve bounds (limited by reserve capacity)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            max_reserve = min(gen_reserve_capacity[i], gen_pmax[i] - gen_pmin[i])\n            bounds.append((0, max_reserve))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n\n    # Find reference bus (bus type 3)\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    for i, bus in enumerate(bus_data):\n        if bus[1] == 3:  # Type 3 = reference bus\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        ref_bus_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Angle bounds (reference bus = 0, others unbounded)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if i == ref_bus_idx:  # Reference bus\n            bounds.append((0, 0))\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Constraints\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n    A_ub = []\n    b_ub = []\n\n    # 1. Power balance at each bus: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) = B * theta\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n        # Generator injections\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[gen_idx] == bus_idx:\n                row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n\n        # Network flow: -sum(B_ij * theta_j)\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            row[2*n_gen + j] = -B[bus_idx, j]\n\n        A_eq.append(row)\n        b_eq.append(bus_load[bus_idx])\n\n    # 2. System reserve requirement: sum(r) >= Reserve_Req\n    row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    row[n_gen:2*n_gen] = -1.0  # Negative for >= constraint converted to <=\n    A_ub.append(row)\n    b_ub.append(-reserve_requirement)\n\n    # 3. Generator capacity coupling: pg + r <= pmax\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if online_gens[i]:\n            row = np.zeros(n_vars)\n            row[i] = 1.0  # pg\n            row[n_gen + i] = 1.0  # r\n            A_ub.append(row)\n            b_ub.append(gen_pmax[i])\n\n    # 4. Line thermal limits: |P_flow| <= P_max\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        from_bus = int(branch[0])\n        to_bus = int(branch[1])\n        x = branch[3]\n\n        if x == 0 or branch_limits[br_idx] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_idx = bus_to_idx[from_bus]\n        to_idx = bus_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        # P_flow = b * (theta_from - theta_to)\n        # Constraint: b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_pos = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_pos[2*n_gen + from_idx] = b\n        row_pos[2*n_gen + to_idx] = -b\n        A_ub.append(row_pos)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n        # Constraint: -b * (theta_from - theta_to) <= P_max\n        row_neg = np.zeros(n_vars)\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + from_idx] = -b\n        row_neg[2*n_gen + to_idx] = b\n        A_ub.append(row_neg)\n        b_ub.append(branch_limits[br_idx])\n\n    # Convert to arrays\n    A_eq = np.array(A_eq) if A_eq else None\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq) if b_eq else None\n    A_ub = np.array(A_ub) if A_ub else None\n    b_ub = np.array(b_ub) if b_ub else None\n\n    # Solve LP\n    result = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,\n                     bounds=bounds, method='highs')\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return None\n\n    # Extract results\n    pg = result.x[0:n_gen]\n    reserves = result.x[n_gen:2*n_gen]\n    theta = result.x[2*n_gen:2*n_gen+n_bus]\n\n    # Calculate LMPs (dual values from power balance constraints)\n    # LMP = marginal cost at each bus\n    lmps = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    # For linear program, use dual values if available\n    if hasattr(result, 'ineqlin') and result.ineqlin is not None:\n        # Extract dual values (shadow prices)\n        pass\n\n    # Simple approximation: use generator marginal costs at each bus\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        gen_at_bus = [i for i in range(n_gen) if gen_bus_idx[i] == bus_idx and online_gens[i]]\n        if gen_at_bus:\n            # Use average marginal cost of generators at this bus\n            lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in gen_at_bus])\n        else:\n            # Use system marginal cost\n            active_gens = [i for i in range(n_gen) if online_gens[i] and pg[i] > 0.01]\n            if active_gens:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean([gen_cost_c1[i] for i in active_gens])\n            else:\n                lmps[bus_idx] = np.mean(gen_cost_c1[online_gens])\n\n    #
